Aerial Camera Mode

Movement Smoothing
Adjust the smoothness of the ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Aerial Camera^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^'s movements.\n\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Low Value^7: Aerial Camera movement is smoother with extra movement added to the observer's inputs, resulting in more progressive accelerations and decelerations.\n\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^High Value^7: Aerial Camera movement is sharper and reflects the observer's movement inputs more closely.

The Armory holds your collection of Custom Mods and Blueprint weapons. These Weapons may be applied directly to your loadouts.
Custom Mods are earned by leveling your Weapons. Each one is a save slot where you can customize and name your firearms for quick access.
Blueprint Weapons are firearms with pre-configured attachments and custom cosmetics. Any Blueprints that you acquire will be shown in the Armory, and can be taken into Gunsmith for modification.
Blueprint weapons are firearms with pre-configured\nattachments and custom cosmetics. Blueprints can be\npurchased in the ^5Store ^7, via the ^5Battle Pass^7, or unlocked in ^5Missions & Challenges^7.\nBlueprints will be shown in the armory and can be taken into Gunsmith for modification.
War Tracks
Unlock War Tracks by earning them in the Battle Pass or purchasing them in the Store.
Equip War Tracks in the Vehicle Customization menu in the Weapons tab.
Hop into the vehicle you equipped your War Track on and use [{ui_secondary}] to turn it on and off..
Store Blueprints
Edit loadout has been updated to show available blueprints that you can obtain from the ^5Store^7 to build out your blueprint collection. 
Blueprints obtainable through the ^5Store ^7are marked with a ^5Blueprint Tag^7. Find these blueprints in the ^5Blueprint Store ^7menu or the ^5Armory ^7menu.
Eliminate Opponents
High Value Target
How To Win
Score-based game mode with respawns where the goal is to kill the other team more than they kill you.
During the fight the top player on each team is marked as a High Value Target. This H.V.T. is worth extra score when killed.
Kill the enemy team and focus on taking down their H.V.T. for extra score. If you find yourself the H.V.T. do your best to stick with your teammates and survive!

Edit Loadout allows you to configure loadouts that can be accessed during the match. Assemble battle-ready combinations of Weapons, Equipment, and Perks.
Primary and Secondary weapons form the base of your loadout. Perks give your soldier abilities on the battlefield.
You may create and name multiple loadouts for various gameplay situations. Be sure to experiment with new combinations as you level up and unlock new functionalities.
Officer Progression
Officer Ranks are a new seasonal leveling system for Call of Duty\xc2\xae: Black Ops 7. Once you have completed the Enlisted Ranks, you may climb the Officer Ranks to earn recognition and rewards.
Each new Officer Rank unlocks a challenge which can be completed for a medal ribbon and an XP boost. The more Rank Challenges you complete, the more ribbons you earn and the faster you progress.
Officer Ranks are specific to the current Season. Climb as high as possible during the current season to maximize your Rank. Remember - Officer Ranks are reset at the start of each new Season, but your achievements will be saved to build your history as a combatant.
Field Upgrades are abilities and pieces of gear that can give you a tactical edge in battle.
They recharge throughout a fight. More powerful Field Upgrades take longer to charge.
Field Upgrade Pro lets you equip two Field Upgrades instead of one. Choose between them on each activation. Unlocked at level 45.
Field upgrade pro is now available. You can now equip 2 field upgrades instead of 1. Choose between them on each activation.
Small team, multiple round battles
Eliminate all enemies to win
Or, first team to reach score limit wins
Gunsmith is a deep weapon customization system that allows you to create your own custom firearms.
Leveling up your weapons in battle will unlock new attachments across 8 attachment zones, as well as weapon perks.
Upgrade your weapon with up to 5 attachments that significantly change how your weapon feels. Experiment with various combinations to suit your playstyle and different game modes.
Gunsmith Customs
When selecting attachments, hit the Blueprint button to select from the attachment variants you own!
Combine cosmetic attachment variants from up to 5 weapons to create the weapon that's right for you!
Intel Missions
New Intel Missions are available to play in Warzone. Find more information in the Missions menu on the Intel tab.
Intel Missions give you a variety of scavenger based missions to do when dropping into the Warzone.
Completing the Intel Missions will give you the chance to follow Ghost and learn more about Verdansk.
Killstreaks are powerful support packages that you can call into the battlefield during a match. Used properly, they can turn the tide of a fight.
Earn Killstreaks by getting Kills without dying. Each type of Killstreak requires a specific number of Kills to earn. The more Kills one costs, the more powerful it is. You may choose three Killstreaks as part of your combat readiness.
When you're in combat, you may trigger each Killstreak once you've achieved the corresponding number of Kills. Be sure to keep an eye on the Killstreak HUD to see what you have available.
Killstreaks
Locate The Objective
Secure The Package
Each round the location of the package is randomized. Move in and attempt to secure.
Eliminate the other enemy team or be holding the package when the round is over to win the round.
Downed teammates can be revived one time. First team to hit the round score limit wins!
Edit loadout allows you to configure your own battle-ready combinations of Weapons, Equipment, Perks, Killstreaks, and Field Upgrades.
Create A Class allows you to configure your own battle-ready combinations of Weapons, Equipment, Perks, Munitions, and Roles.
Primary and Secondary weapons form the base of your loadout. Perks give your soldier special abilities on the battlefield, while Killstreaks and Field Upgrades allow you to call in support mid-match.
Primary and Secondary weapons form the base of your class. Perks give your soldier special abilities on the battlefield, while Munitions and Roles allow you provide support to your team.
You may create and name multiple loadouts for various gameplay situations. Be sure to experiment with new combinations as you level up and unlock new functionality. You can set your favorite loadout to see it on the gun bench and in the hands of your Operator in the menus.

Mission Challenges
Missions are multi-step challenges that can be completed for XP bonuses and special rewards.
Missions must be activated in order to complete them. Activate a Mission from the Missions page and track your progress. You will be notified when you complete each step and when you have completed your overall objectives.
There are several Missions available, with more to be released in each new season. Be sure to check back to the Missions page for new challenges and rewards.
EndGame AfterMath
Custom Mods are weapons that you create, customize, and save into your Armory. Mod slots are unlocked by leveling each weapon.
Create a Custom Mod in Gunsmith by adding attachments, customizing with camos and charms, and then saving it into one of your Mod Slots. You can personalize its name to make it stand out.
Custom Mods track stats, so your weapon will build a history. As long as your Mod retains the same base weapon and name, you may continue to modify it.

Perks have been adjusted to help create more balanced choices. Weak and unpopular perks have been buffed! Read the notes in the perk descriptions to find out more about what has changed for each perk.

^5Kill Chain ^7has been moved to the blue slot. Combine it with Hardline or Pointman for a super charged killstreak loadout.

Attack And Defend
Secure And Extract
Round based mode where attackers must locate and exfil Prisoners being held by the defending team.
Secured Prisoners must be carried to extraction. Teammates who are downed can be revived one time. Carrying a prisoner provides a radar sweep to the team.
Eliminating the enemy team is 50 points and every prisoner extracted or held at round end is worth 100 points. First team to 500 points wins!
A Role is defined by the combination of an activated ability and a passive ability.
Choose a Role for your desired play style.
A team with players in different Roles is equipped to handle any situation.

Specialist Mode Available
Not Now
Toggle Specialist Mode
At level 29 the specialist mode becomes available. When activated you can have up to 6 perks in your loadout. You earn an extra perk every 2 kills. After 8 kills you earn the Specialist bonus which grants most perks and weapon perks in the game.
*Killstreaks are disabled when this mode is active

Motion Sensor Function Steadying Initial Scale
Set the ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^initial value^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ from which the ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^motion sensor function steadying's scale is calculated^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ . The scale used to reduce slow rotation goes from this value with no input and progressively goes back up to one as the speed gets closer to the motion sensor function steadying threshold.\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ Increasing this value weakens the motion sensor function's steadying effect.
Motion Sensor Function Steadying Threshold
Set the ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^controller rotation speed^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ at which motion sensor function steadying stops reducing input, ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^in degrees per second^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^. Motion sensor function steadying helps ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^reduce jitter^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ due to small motion inputs by reducing the effect resulting from controller rotation slower than the threshold.\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ Setting this to zero disables steadying.

Mouse Advanced Settings
Group of advanced mouse settings.
Group of settings to calibrate the mouse.
Mouse Filtering
Set how much mouse input of the previous frames is taken into consideration on the current frame.\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ Only recommended to sacrifice precision for normalized movement.
Monitor Distance Coefficient
Set an on-screen distance where the relation between mouse hardware and screen movement speed is constant across any zoom level. \n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ Value is based on a 16:9 screen monitor.
Mouse Smoothing
Smooths aim movement based on the two last frames' average.\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ Only recommended to sacrifice precision for normalized movements.
Vertical Sensitivity Multiplier
Set a level of multiplication to the ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Mouse Sensitivity^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ for the ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^vertical^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ axis only.\n\nLeaving the multiplier at 1.00 gives the same sensitivity for vertical and horizontal axes.
Mouse Wheel Delay
Set the minimum time in milliseconds between consecutive mouse wheel inputs before another signal is sent.\n\nLow values will make it easier to trigger an action twice in quick succession if it is bound to the mouse wheel. This can also make it more likely to accidentally trigger the action more times than intended.\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ By default, scrolling the mouse wheel is used to switch weapons.
Mouse Acceleration

NVIDIA DLSS Multi-frame Generation
2X
3X
4X
DLSS Multi-Frame Generation uses ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^AI frames^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ to boost frame rate by generating up to three additional frames and optimizes responsiveness with NVIDIA Reflex.\n\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^2x:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Generate one additional frame between engine frames for good FPS gain.\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^3x:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Generate two additional frames between engine frames for higher FPS gain.\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^4x:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Generate three additional frames between engine frames for maximum FPS gain.\n\nNote: ^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^Requires a GeForce RTX 5000-series GPU.
The ^BNVIDIA DLSS Frame Generation^R may be unavailable because GPU scheduling is disabled. Please refer to the Windows documentation to activate it.

NVIDIA Reflex Low Latency
NVIDIA Reflex is used to reduce system latency which increases PC responsiveness. \n\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Off:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Low Latency mode is disabled.\n\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^On:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Low Latency mode is enabled and optimizing system latency.\n\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^On + Boost:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Low Latency mode is now enabled and optimizing system latency. Additionally, GPU clock frequencies are kept high in CPU-bound cases. This can reduce latency but will increase GPU power draw.\n

Basic
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Basic:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Set most quality settings to low values to obtain a high count of frames per second (FPS).
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Custom:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Manually defined quality settings values.
Extreme

Minimum
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Minimum:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Set all quality settings to their minimum value to obtain the highest count of frames per second (FPS).

^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Quality:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Optimizes quality settings values according to your hardware to reach the best visual quality.

Restart Shaders Pre-Loading
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Deletes the shaders cache and pre-load all shaders^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ from the beginning. Shaders pre-loading prevents performance issues when playing on a map. This process only needs to be done once unless your display adapter's drivers have changed.\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ Restarting the game is required for changes to take effect.
Shaders Pre-Loading Info
Pre-loading shaders is an essential step for your hardware to display the game. If they get paused, you put yourself at ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^risk of getting performance issues^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ that might cause disconnections, lag, and even crashes.
^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^Pre-loading shaders is an essential step for your hardware to display the game. They work alongside your installed drivers and display adapter to improve your ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^in-game performance ^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^and guarantee all textures are displayed correctly.\n\n&&1
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^You must complete the shaders pre-loading^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ to avoid any risk of performance issues, instabilities or crashes when playing a map for the first time. Skipping pre-loading shaders might display the following icons while in-game:

Upscaling / Sharpening
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^FidelityFX Contrast Adaptive Sharpening (CAS):^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Enhance the sharpness of the image.\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ It is recommended to turn off any sharpening techniques on your graphics card driver for this setting to work as expected.\n
Select a Upscale/Sharpen solution to maximize performance and/or sharpen the image.\n\n&&1\n&&2
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^NVIDIA DLSS:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ NVIDIA RTX technology which uses a deep learning neural network to boost frame rates and generate beautiful, sharp images.
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^AMD FidelityFX Super Resolution 1 (FSR):^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Cutting edge super-optimized spatial upscaling technology that produces impressive image quality at fast frame rates.
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^AMD FidelityFX Super Resolution &&1 (FSR):^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Cutting edge upscaling technique developed from the ground up to produce high resolution frames from lower resolution inputs.\n
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^AMD FSR &&1:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ AMD Fidelity FX Super Resolution &&2 combines high-quality image upscaling with frame generation technologies to generate high resolution frames at fast frame rates.\n
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^NVIDIA Image Scaling (NIS):^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Offers the best spatial scaling and sharpening and supports all graphic cards.
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Intel XeSS:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ Super Sampling technology using machine learning to deliver more performance with exceptional image quality.

Variable Rate Shading
Adjusts the level of detail in different areas of the screen according to their importance. This improves performance while maintaining visual quality.\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ This setting requires a reboot of the game to take effect.

V-Sync (Gameplay)
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Limits frame rate^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ to match the refresh rate of the monitor ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^while in gameplay^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^.\n\nSelect a value lower than 100% to cap the V-Sync to a fraction of the monitor frame rate capacity. (E.g. 50% of a 120hz monitor will limit V-Sync to 1/2 of its full capacity, i.e. 60hz.)\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ Enabling this setting avoids screen tearing problems, but induces some input lag.\n
^BNVIDIA DLSS Frame Generation^R is set to ^BOn^R.^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^

Flick Stick Activation
The flick stick control scheme allows more control of the horizontal axis, and needs to be paired with motion sensor function for the vertical aim and horizontal adjustments.\n\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Off:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ The flick stick control scheme is disabled.\n\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Motion Sensor Function Only:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ The flick stick control scheme is enabled when the motion sensor function is active.\n\n^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^On:^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ The flick stick control scheme is enabled at all times.
Flick Stick Turn Smoothing Threshold   
A smoothing is applied to turn inputs after the initial flick smaller than this value, reducing jitter. \n\nIncreasing this value extends the smoothing to faster stick rotation.
Flick Stick Threshold
The stick deflection required to initiate a flick.
Flick Duration
The amount of time needed to complete a flick.\n\nIncreasing the value will make it longer to complete the flick.\n

All Perks in Black Ops 7 are classified into 3 Combat Specialties: ^?#x091803ab3da793832.#x06a0929c87a48cd3c^Enforcer^7, ^?#x091803ab3da793832.#x0bd01b8eb88db21bf^Recon^7, or ^?#x091803ab3da793832.#x0b43d246ee232fad6^Strategist.
Equip 3 Perks of the same Combat Specialty (color) to activate a Core bonus.
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^New in Black Ops 7: ^7Mix and match perks from two different Combat Specialties (colors) to activate a Hybrid bonus.
